For example a survival mod adds \"Decrease Movement -20 / -40 / -60\" for each stage of tiredness: while you are not tired they are all switched off and do not slow you; a lantern mod adds \"Is Daytime\" and \"Not Daytime\": only one of them is on at a time.\n\nWhen the condition becomes true (you get tired, night falls, you start sneaking...), the game switches the effect on by itself, and off again when it stops being true.\n\nA switched-off effect does NOT change any stat, speed or damage, so it is not a problem and is not \"stuck\". There is no need to remove it, and removing it would not help: the ability or mod that gave it adds it again. Only effects that say \"running\" act on your character."
tr "已关闭：此效果在角色上，但当前未起任何作用，因为此刻其条件不满足。\n\n许多模组和能力会一次性添加一整套效果，每个都有自己的条件，游戏只会开启当前条件成立的那些。例如某个生存模组为每个疲劳阶段添加「移动速度 -20 / -40 / -60」：当你不疲劳时它们全都关闭，不会拖慢你；某个提灯模组添加「白天」和「非白天」：同时只有一个开启。\n\n当条件变为成立时（你变得疲劳、夜幕降临、开始潜行……），游戏会自行开启该效果，条件不再成立时又会关闭。\n\n已关闭的效果不会改变任何属性、速度或伤害，因此不是问题，也不是「卡住」。无需移除，移除也没有帮助：给予它的能力或模组会重新添加。只有标为「运行中」的效果才会作用于角色。"

# AppStarfield.cpp
en "A plugin was flagged light or medium (or the flag was removed) after this save was made. When a save loads, Starfield rebuilds every saved form id for today's plugins: a light plugin keeps only the last 3 hex digits of an id, a medium one the last 4 (Starfield.exe 1.16.244, TESFile::AdjustFormID). The save's ids of these plugins that are larger than that now point at other records of the plugin, or at none: placed objects, quests and NPC changes of the mod go to the wrong things."
tr "此存档创建后，某个插件被标记为轻量或中型（或取消了该标记）。加载存档时，Starfield 会按当前插件重建每个已保存的表单 ID：轻量插件只保留 ID 的最后 3 位十六进制数，中型插件保留最后 4 位（Starfield.exe 1.16.244，TESFile::AdjustFormID）。这些插件在存档中超出该范围的 ID 现在指向该插件的其他记录或不指向任何记录：该模组的放置对象、任务和 NPC 更改会落到错误的对象上。"

# AppStarfield.cpp
en "\nThe fix is outside the save: give the plugin back the kind it had when you saved (undo the light / medium flag, or reinstall the mod's earlier version), or load a save made after the change.
